HIGH SCHOOL — Woodlawn High, Birmingham, Ala 
COLLEGE — Howard (now Samford) 1953 


COLLEGIATE FOOTBALL EXPERIENCE — University of Alabama (G8), 
freshman; Howard (QB), sophomore-senior 


GRADUATE DEGREE — Peabody Collage 


WIFE — The former Julia Ann Estock

HEAD COACHING HONORS — 1977 Southern Independent the 
Year; 1979 National Coach of the Year (ABC-Chevrole!); 1979 Southern 
Independent Coach of the Year; 1980 National Coach of the Year (Bobby 
Dodd); 1983 Florida Sports Hall of Fame; 1986 Alabama Sports Hall of 

ich ofthe Year; 1997 Walter Camp Coach of the 
Year; 1992 Neyland Trophy; 1993 Bear Bryant Award Finalist; 1993 ACC 
Coach of the Year
